# Agents · R · part 18

1,014 entries, review-lite to reviewer. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **review-lite**: Lightweight correctness reviewer for small diffs. Delegates here for correctness-gap review of changes under 300 LOC: logic errors, broken edge cases, unhandled failures, contract mismatches. Read-only by capability (no Bash, no Write/Edit). Use when a small change needs an independent verifier before merge. For diffs of 300 LOC or more, or for style, architecture, or multi-perspective review, delegate to review; for deep security audits delegate to security. ([lx-wnk/agents](https://github.com/lx-wnk/agents/blob/HEAD/agents/review-lite.md) · ★1)
- **review-logic**: Review logical consistency, argument structure, and contradiction detection in document content. Dispatched by the reviewer-gate during the review phase.
